// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

codex
The key dependency is the greedy growth within one seed cluster; different seeds in the same QT round are independent. I’m preserving deterministic tie-breaking while targeting that seed-level parallelism, and I’m also checking whether repeated distance work can be eliminated without changing the greedy decisions.

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the

+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {

The hot path is now seed-parallel with a persistent OpenMP team. Candidate growth uses precomputed distances and monotonic eligibility filtering while retaining original ordering and tie rules.

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
-        }
     }

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }

+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),

+            // Candidate costs vary with local point density; guided scheduling
+            // balances dense seed neighborhoods without excessive dispatch cost.

+

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                for (int t = 0; t < team_size; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best[t].seed < 0) continue;
+                    if (winner < 0 ||

+

+                    }

+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),

+                }
             }

-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;

     }

+
     return clusters;

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }

+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = -1;
+            local_best.members.clear();
+            const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+
+            // Candidate costs vary with local point density; guided scheduling
+            // balances dense seed neighborhoods without excessive dispatch cost.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(guided, 1)

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                for (int t = 0; t < team_size; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best[t].seed < 0) continue;
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_best[t].cardinality > thread_best[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best[t].cardinality == thread_best[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_best[t].seed < thread_best[winner].seed)) {
+                        winner = t;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_best[winner].cardinality > 0) {

+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_best[winner].seed;
+                    cluster.members = thread_best[winner].members;
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),

+                }
             }

+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }

+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),

+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.

+            // balances dense seed neighborhoods without excessive dispatch cost.

+                    if (winner < 0 ||

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

+// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
+// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
+// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
+// member instead of rescanning all previous members.

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
     }

+// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
+// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
+// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
+// member instead of rescanning all previous members.
+int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
+                             const std::vector<uint8_t>& clustered,
+                             const DistanceData& distances,
+                             const double threshold,
+                             CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {

+    std::vector<int>& eligible = workspace.eligible_points;

+        members.push_back(newest_member);
+        eligible.erase(eligible.begin() + closest_slot);
+        max_distances.erase(max_distances.begin() + closest_slot);
+
+        const double* newest_distances = distances.row(newest_member);
+        size_t write = 0;
+        closest_slot = -1;
+        min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+

+        // original winner when two candidates have the same diameter.

+                max_distances[write] = max_dist;

+                    closest_slot = static_cast<int>(write);

     }

+    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT rounds.  This avoids repeatedly

+    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(omp_get_max_threads());

+    #pragma omp parallel shared(finished, thread_best, unclustered_indices, clustered, clusters)
+    {
+        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
+        CandidateWorkspace workspace;
+        workspace.reserve(N);
+
+        while (true) {
+            #pragma omp single
+            finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
+
+            if (finished) break;
+
+            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[thread_id];
+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = -1;
+            local_best.members.clear();
+            const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+
+            // Candidate costs vary sharply with local point density. Unit-sized
+            // dynamic work distribution prevents dense seeds from stranding a
+            // thread while the rest of the team waits at the round barrier.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {

+                    local_best.seed = seed;

+            // The ordered tie break makes the reduction independent of thread

+                int winner = -1;
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                for (int t = 0; t < team_size; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best[t].seed < 0) continue;
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_best[t].cardinality > thread_best[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best[t].cardinality == thread_best[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_best[t].seed < thread_best[winner].seed)) {
+                        winner = t;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_best[winner].cardinality > 0) {

+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_best[winner].seed;
+                    cluster.members = thread_best[winner].members;

+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));

+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());

+                    unclustered_indices.clear();
+                }
             }

-                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
-                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
-                unclustered_indices.end()

-            // No more clusters can be formed

Exact result hashes now match the sequential binary for 120–1000 points, 1–32 threads, and thresholds from 0.75 through 25. Dynamic seed scheduling also outperformed guided scheduling at higher thread counts, so I’ve retained it and am doing the final sanitizer/build/validation pass.

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

+// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
+// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
+// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
+// member instead of rescanning all previous members.
+int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
+                             const std::vector<uint8_t>& clustered,
+                             const DistanceData& distances,
+                             const double threshold,
+                             CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
+    std::vector<int>& members = workspace.members;
+    std::vector<int>& eligible = workspace.eligible_points;
+    std::vector<double>& max_distances = workspace.max_distances;

-

+    // Every member of a valid cluster must be within the threshold of its
+    // seed, so the precomputed seed neighborhood is the complete initial set.
+    const double* seed_distances = distances.row(seed_point);
+    int closest_slot = -1;
+    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+    for (const int candidate : distances.neighbors(seed_point)) {
+        if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+
+        const double dist = seed_distances[candidate];
+        eligible.push_back(candidate);
+        max_distances.push_back(dist);

+        members.push_back(newest_member);
+        eligible.erase(eligible.begin() + closest_slot);
+        max_distances.erase(max_distances.begin() + closest_slot);
+
+        const double* newest_distances = distances.row(newest_member);
+        size_t write = 0;
+        closest_slot = -1;
+        min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+        // Filtering in place retains ascending point order and therefore the
+        // original winner when two candidates have the same diameter.
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < eligible.size(); ++read) {
+            const int candidate = eligible[read];
+            const double max_dist = std::max(max_distances[read],
+                                             newest_distances[candidate]);
+            if (max_dist < threshold) {
+                eligible[write] = candidate;
+                max_distances[write] = max_dist;
+                if (max_dist < min_diameter) {
+                    min_diameter = max_dist;
+                    closest_slot = static_cast<int>(write);

+                ++write;
+            }
+        }
+        eligible.resize(write);
+        max_distances.resize(write);
     }
-    
+
     return static_cast<int>(members.size());

+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));

+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);

+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {

+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);

+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }

+// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
+// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
+// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
+// member instead of rescanning all previous members.
+int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
+                             const std::vector<uint8_t>& clustered,
+                             const DistanceData& distances,
+                             const double threshold,
+                             CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {

+        members.push_back(newest_member);
+        eligible.erase(eligible.begin() + closest_slot);
+        max_distances.erase(max_distances.begin() + closest_slot);
+
+        const double* newest_distances = distances.row(newest_member);
+        size_t write = 0;

+        // original winner when two candidates have the same diameter.

+                    if (winner < 0 ||

     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP shared-memory version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <chrono>
     9	#include <cmath>

    23	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    24	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    25	
    26	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    27	struct Point {
    28	    double x, y;
    29	};
    30	
    31	// Structure to represent a cluster
    32	struct Cluster {
    33	    std::vector<int> members;
    34	    int seed_point;
    35	};
    36	
    37	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    38	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    39	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    40	    
    41	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    42	    int count = 0;
    43	    
    44	    while (count < N) {
    45	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    46	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    47	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    48	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    49	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    50	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    51	        
    52	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    53	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    54	            group_cnt = N - count;
    55	        }
    56	        
    57	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    58	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    59	            const double r = frand() * R;
    60	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    61	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    62	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    63	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    64	            
    65	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    66	                continue;
    67	            }
    68	            
    69	            points[count] = {x, y};
    70	            count++;
    71	            group_cnt--;
    72	        }
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    77	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    78	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    79	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    80	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    81	}
    82	
    83	// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
    84	// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
    85	// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
    86	// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
    87	class DistanceData {
    88	  public:
    89	    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
    90	        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
    91	          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
    92	          neighbors_(point_count_) {
    93	        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
    94	        const double area_fraction = std::min(
    95	            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
    96	        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
    97	            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
    98	            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
    99	
   100	        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
   101	        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
   102	        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   103	        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
   104	            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
   105	            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
   106	            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
   107	
   108	            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
   109	                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
   110	                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
   111	                row[candidate] = dist;
   112	                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
   113	                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
   114	                }
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    const double* row(const int member) const {
   120	        return values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    const std::vector<int>& neighbors(const int point) const {
   124	        return neighbors_[point];
   125	    }
   126	
   127	  private:
   128	    int point_count_;
   129	    std::unique_ptr<double[]> values_;
   130	    std::vector<std::vector<int>> neighbors_;
   131	};
   132	
   133	struct CandidateWorkspace {
   134	    std::vector<int> members;
   135	    std::vector<int> eligible_points;
   136	    std::vector<double> max_distances;
   137	
   138	    void reserve(const size_t point_count) {
   139	        members.reserve(point_count);
   140	        eligible_points.reserve(point_count);
   141	        max_distances.reserve(point_count);
   142	    }
   143	};
   144	
   145	// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
   146	// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
   147	// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
   148	// member instead of rescanning all previous members.
   149	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
   150	                             const std::vector<uint8_t>& clustered,
   151	                             const DistanceData& distances,
   152	                             const double threshold,
   153	                             CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {
   154	    std::vector<int>& members = workspace.members;
   155	    std::vector<int>& eligible = workspace.eligible_points;
   156	    std::vector<double>& max_distances = workspace.max_distances;
   157	    members.clear();
   158	    eligible.clear();
   159	    max_distances.clear();
   160	    members.push_back(seed_point);
   161	
   162	    // Every member of a valid cluster must be within the threshold of its
   163	    // seed, so the precomputed seed neighborhood is the complete initial set.
   164	    const double* seed_distances = distances.row(seed_point);
   165	    int closest_slot = -1;
   166	    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   167	    for (const int candidate : distances.neighbors(seed_point)) {
   168	        if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
   169	
   170	        const double dist = seed_distances[candidate];
   171	        eligible.push_back(candidate);
   172	        max_distances.push_back(dist);
   173	        if (dist < min_diameter) {
   174	            min_diameter = dist;
   175	            closest_slot = static_cast<int>(eligible.size()) - 1;
   176	        }
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    while (closest_slot >= 0) {
   180	        const int newest_member = eligible[closest_slot];
   181	        members.push_back(newest_member);
   182	        eligible.erase(eligible.begin() + closest_slot);
   183	        max_distances.erase(max_distances.begin() + closest_slot);
   184	
   185	        const double* newest_distances = distances.row(newest_member);
   186	        size_t write = 0;
   187	        closest_slot = -1;
   188	        min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
   189	
   190	        // Filtering in place retains ascending point order and therefore the
   191	        // original winner when two candidates have the same diameter.
   192	        for (size_t read = 0; read < eligible.size(); ++read) {
   193	            const int candidate = eligible[read];
   194	            const double max_dist = std::max(max_distances[read],
   195	                                             newest_distances[candidate]);
   196	            if (max_dist < threshold) {
   197	                eligible[write] = candidate;
   198	                max_distances[write] = max_dist;
   199	                if (max_dist < min_diameter) {
   200	                    min_diameter = max_dist;
   201	                    closest_slot = static_cast<int>(write);
   202	                }
   203	                ++write;
   204	            }
   205	        }
   206	        eligible.resize(write);
   207	        max_distances.resize(write);
   208	    }
   209	
   210	    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
   211	}
   212	
   213	struct ThreadBest {
   214	    int cardinality = -1;
   215	    int seed = -1;
   216	    std::vector<int> members;
   217	};
   218	
   219	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   220	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   221	                                  const double threshold) {
   222	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   223	    const DistanceData distances(points, threshold);
   224	    std::vector<uint8_t> clustered(N, 0);
   225	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices(N);
   226	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   227	    clusters.reserve(N);
   228	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   229	        unclustered_indices[i] = i;
   230	    }
   231	
   232	    // Keep one OpenMP team alive across all QT rounds.  This avoids repeatedly
   233	    // creating a team as the active point set shrinks.
   234	    std::vector<ThreadBest> thread_best(omp_get_max_threads());
   235	    bool finished = false;
   236	
   237	    #pragma omp parallel shared(finished, thread_best, unclustered_indices, clustered, clusters)
   238	    {
   239	        const int thread_id = omp_get_thread_num();
   240	        CandidateWorkspace workspace;
   241	        workspace.reserve(N);
   242	
   243	        while (true) {
   244	            #pragma omp single
   245	            finished = unclustered_indices.empty();
   246	
   247	            if (finished) break;
   248	
   249	            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[thread_id];
   250	            local_best.cardinality = -1;
   251	            local_best.seed = -1;
   252	            local_best.members.clear();
   253	            const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   254	
   255	            // Candidate costs vary sharply with local point density. Unit-sized
   256	            // dynamic work distribution prevents dense seeds from stranding a
   257	            // thread while the rest of the team waits at the round barrier.
   258	            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   259	            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
   260	                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   261	                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
   262	                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, workspace);
   263	
   264	                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
   265	                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
   266	                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
   267	                    local_best.seed = seed;
   268	                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
   269	                }
   270	            }
   271	
   272	            // The ordered tie break makes the reduction independent of thread
   273	            // count and scheduling, matching the original ascending seed scan.
   274	            #pragma omp single
   275	            {
   276	                int winner = -1;
   277	                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
   278	                for (int t = 0; t < team_size; ++t) {
   279	                    if (thread_best[t].seed < 0) continue;
   280	                    if (winner < 0 ||
   281	                        thread_best[t].cardinality > thread_best[winner].cardinality ||
   282	                        (thread_best[t].cardinality == thread_best[winner].cardinality &&
   283	                         thread_best[t].seed < thread_best[winner].seed)) {
   284	                        winner = t;
   285	                    }
   286	                }
   287	
   288	                if (winner >= 0 && thread_best[winner].cardinality > 0) {
   289	                    Cluster cluster;
   290	                    cluster.seed_point = thread_best[winner].seed;
   291	                    cluster.members = thread_best[winner].members;
   292	                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
   293	                        clustered[member] = 1;
   294	                    }
   295	                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
   296	
   297	                    unclustered_indices.erase(
   298	                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
   299	                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
   300	                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
   301	                                       }),
   302	                        unclustered_indices.end());
   303	                } else {
   304	                    unclustered_indices.clear();
   305	                }
   306	            }
   307	        }
   308	    }
   309	
   310	    return clusters;
   311	}

   313	// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
   314	bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
   315	                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
   316	                     const double threshold) {
   317	    bool valid = true;
   318	    
   319	    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
   320	    
   321	    // Check each cluster
   322	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   323	        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
   324	        double max_diameter = 0.0;
   325	        
   326	        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
   327	        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
   328	            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
   329	                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
   330	                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
   331	                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
   332	            }
   333	        }
   334	        
   335	        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
   336	            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
   337	                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
   338	        }
   339	        
   340	        // Validate diameter is within threshold
   341	        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
   342	            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
   343	                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
   344	            valid = false;
   345	        }
   346	    }
   347	    
   348	    // Check for duplicate memberships
   349	    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
   350	    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
   351	        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
   352	            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
   353	            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
   354	                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
   355	                       member, membership[member], c);
   356	                valid = false;
   357	            }
   358	            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
   359	        }
   360	    }

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

+// Generate one greedy candidate cluster.  A candidate's diameter can only
+// increase as members are added, so points that reach the threshold are
+// permanently removed.  The running maximum is updated using only the newest
+// member instead of rescanning all previous members.
+int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
+                             const std::vector<uint8_t>& clustered,
+                             const DistanceData& distances,
+                             const double threshold,
+                             CandidateWorkspace& workspace) {

+    std::vector<int>& eligible = workspace.eligible_points;

+    // Every member of a valid cluster must be within the threshold of its
+    // seed, so the precomputed seed neighborhood is the complete initial set.
+    const double* seed_distances = distances.row(seed_point);
+    int closest_slot = -1;
+    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+    for (const int candidate : distances.neighbors(seed_point)) {
+        if (clustered[candidate]) continue;
+
+        const double dist = seed_distances[candidate];
+        eligible.push_back(candidate);
+        max_distances.push_back(dist);

+        members.push_back(newest_member);
+        eligible.erase(eligible.begin() + closest_slot);
+        max_distances.erase(max_distances.begin() + closest_slot);
+
+        const double* newest_distances = distances.row(newest_member);
+        size_t write = 0;
+        closest_slot = -1;
+        min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+
+        // Filtering in place retains ascending point order and therefore the
+        // original winner when two candidates have the same diameter.
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < eligible.size(); ++read) {
+            const int candidate = eligible[read];
+            const double max_dist = std::max(max_distances[read],
+                                             newest_distances[candidate]);
+            if (max_dist < threshold) {
+                eligible[write] = candidate;
+                max_distances[write] = max_dist;
+                if (max_dist < min_diameter) {
+                    min_diameter = max_dist;
+                    closest_slot = static_cast<int>(write);

+            }
+        }
+        eligible.resize(write);
+        max_distances.resize(write);
     }
-

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
-        }
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }

+            if (finished) break;
+
+            ThreadBest& local_best = thread_best[thread_id];
+            local_best.cardinality = -1;
+            local_best.seed = -1;
+            local_best.members.clear();
+            const int active_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+
+            // Candidate costs vary sharply with local point density. Unit-sized
+            // dynamic work distribution prevents dense seeds from stranding a
+            // thread while the rest of the team waits at the round barrier.
+            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+            for (int i = 0; i < active_count; ++i) {
+                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(
+                    seed, clustered, distances, threshold, workspace);
+
+                if (cardinality > local_best.cardinality ||
+                    (cardinality == local_best.cardinality && seed < local_best.seed)) {
+                    local_best.cardinality = cardinality;
+                    local_best.seed = seed;
+                    local_best.members = workspace.members;
+                }
+            }
+
+            // The ordered tie break makes the reduction independent of thread
+            // count and scheduling, matching the original ascending seed scan.
+            #pragma omp single
+            {
+                int winner = -1;
+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();
+                for (int t = 0; t < team_size; ++t) {
+                    if (thread_best[t].seed < 0) continue;
+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_best[t].cardinality > thread_best[winner].cardinality ||
+                        (thread_best[t].cardinality == thread_best[winner].cardinality &&
+                         thread_best[t].seed < thread_best[winner].seed)) {
+                        winner = t;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                if (winner >= 0 && thread_best[winner].cardinality > 0) {
+                    Cluster cluster;
+                    cluster.seed_point = thread_best[winner].seed;
+                    cluster.members = thread_best[winner].members;
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;
+                                       }),
+                        unclustered_indices.end());
+                } else {
+                    unclustered_indices.clear();
+                }
             }

-            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list

-            // No more clusters can be formed
-            break;
         }
     }
-    
+

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);

+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);

+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);

+            local_best.seed = -1;

+            // Candidate costs vary sharply with local point density. Unit-sized
+            // dynamic work distribution prevents dense seeds from stranding a
+            // thread while the rest of the team waits at the round barrier.

+                    local_best.seed = seed;

+            // count and scheduling, matching the original ascending seed scan.

+                const int team_size = omp_get_num_threads();

+                    if (winner < 0 ||
+                        thread_best[t].cardinality > thread_best[winner].cardinality ||

+                    }
+                }
+

+                    cluster.members = thread_best[winner].members;
+                    for (const int member : cluster.members) {
+                        clustered[member] = 1;
+                    }
+                    clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));
+
+                    unclustered_indices.erase(
+                        std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                                       [&clustered](const int idx) {
+                                           return clustered[idx] != 0;

             }

codex
Implemented OpenMP parallelization without adding files or changing the executable name.

Key changes:

- Required OpenMP through [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_omp_r5/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Added parallel distance precomputation and neighborhood indexing.
- Parallelized independent seed evaluation using a persistent OpenMP team and dynamic scheduling.
- Preserved deterministic candidate and seed tie-breaking.
- Eliminated repeated distance and diameter rescans.

Verification:

- Exact membership hashes match the original across multiple sizes, thresholds, and 1–32 threads.
- Validation passes.
- ASan and UBSan pass.
- Representative `n=1000, t=2` timing: original 7012 ms; optimized 34 ms with one thread and 8 ms with 16 threads. Results remain hash-identical.

+// All seed clusters in one QT round read the same point set.  Precomputing the
+// distances once both removes repeated square roots and makes the innermost
+// updates read a contiguous matrix row.  Each neighbor row remains sorted by
+// point index, which preserves the sequential implementation's tie breaking.
+class DistanceData {
+  public:
+    DistanceData(const std::vector<Point>& points, const double threshold)
+        : point_count_(static_cast<int>(points.size())),
+          values_(new double[static_cast<size_t>(point_count_) * point_count_]),
+          neighbors_(point_count_) {
+        constexpr double PI = 3.14159265358979323846;
+        const double area_fraction = std::min(
+            1.0, PI * threshold * threshold / (MAX_WIDTH * MAX_HEIGHT));
+        const size_t estimated_neighbors = std::min(
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ > 0 ? point_count_ - 1 : 0),
+            static_cast<size_t>(point_count_ * area_fraction * 2.0) + 8);
+
+        // Row ownership gives deterministic results and parallel first-touch
+        // placement of the potentially large matrix on NUMA systems.
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int member = 0; member < point_count_; ++member) {
+            double* const row = values_.get() + static_cast<size_t>(member) * point_count_;
+            std::vector<int>& row_neighbors = neighbors_[member];
+            row_neighbors.reserve(estimated_neighbors);
+
+            for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count_; ++candidate) {
+                // Keep the argument order used by the original candidate scan.
+                const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+                row[candidate] = dist;
+                if (candidate != member && dist < threshold) {
+                    row_neighbors.push_back(candidate);
+                }
+            }
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
-        }
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
-}
